        "data-o_data-large_image", "data-zoom-image"];
      var responsive = ["srcset", "data-srcset", "data-lazy-srcset", "data-o_srcset", "data-flickity-lazyload-srcset",
        "sizes", "data-sizes", "data-o_sizes"];
      var scheduled = false;
      function set(node, name, value) {
        if (node.getAttribute(name) !== value) node.setAttribute(name, value);
      }
      function imageLink(value) {
        return /\.(?:avif|gif|jpe?g|png|svg|webp)(?:[?#].*)?$/i.test(value || "") ||
          /^data:image\//i.test(value || "");
      }
      function ancestors(image, value) {
        var parent = image.parentElement;
        for (var depth = 0; parent && depth < 6; depth++, parent = parent.parentElement) {
          if (/^(?:MAIN|HEADER|FOOTER|NAV|FORM|TABLE|TR|TD)$/.test(parent.tagName)) break;
          if (parent.querySelectorAll("img").length !== 1) break;
          // Image-link metadata is safe to update. Never mark arbitrary wrappers
          // as replacement images or apply image sizing to their content.
          if (parent.hasAttribute("data-thumb")) set(parent, "data-thumb", value);
          if (parent.hasAttribute("data-thumb-src")) set(parent, "data-thumb-src", value);
          if (parent.matches("a[href]") && imageLink(parent.getAttribute("href"))) set(parent, "href", value);
        }
      }
      function lock(node, value) {
        set(node, marker, value);
        responsive.forEach(function (name) {
          if (node.tagName !== "SOURCE" || name !== "srcset") node.removeAttribute(name);
        });
        set(node, node.tagName === "SOURCE" ? "srcset" : "src", value);
        aliases.forEach(function (name) {
          if (node.hasAttribute(name)) set(node, name, value);
        });
        if (node.tagName !== "IMG") return;
        var picture = node.parentElement;
        if (picture && picture.tagName === "PICTURE" && picture.querySelectorAll("img").length === 1) {
          Array.prototype.forEach.call(picture.children, function (child) {
            if (child.tagName !== "SOURCE") return;
            child.removeAttribute("type");
            lock(child, value);
          });
        }
        ancestors(node, value);
        // Other gallery images are separate choices. Each explicitly selected leaf
        // is enforced with its own replacement marker in the main loop below.
      }
      function lockPoster(video, value) {
        // The selected graphic remains a VIDEO so its captured layout and responsive
        // selectors still apply. Its old movie must not cover the replacement poster.
        set(video, "poster", value);
        var changed = false;
        function clear(node, names) {
          names.forEach(function (name) {
            if (node.hasAttribute(name)) { node.removeAttribute(name); changed = true; }
          });
        }
        var mediaAttributes = ["src", "data-lazyload"].concat(aliases, responsive);
        var playbackAttributes = mediaAttributes.concat(mediaAttributes.map(function (name) { return "data-transformer-asset-" + name; }));
        clear(video, ["autoplay", "loop", "controls"].concat(playbackAttributes));
        Array.prototype.forEach.call(video.children, function (child) {
          if (child.tagName === "SOURCE") clear(child, playbackAttributes);
        });
        if (!video.paused) video.pause();
        if (changed || video.currentSrc) video.load();
      }
      function enforce() {
        document.querySelectorAll("video[" + marker + "]").forEach(function (video) {
          var value = video.getAttribute(marker);
          if (value) lockPoster(video, value);
        });
        document.querySelectorAll("img,source").forEach(function (node) {
          if (node.tagName === "SOURCE" && node.parentElement && node.parentElement.matches("video[" + marker + "]")) return;
          var value = node.getAttribute(marker);
          if (!value) {
            ["src", "srcset"].concat(aliases).some(function (name) {
              var candidate = node.getAttribute(name);
              if (candidate && /(?:^|\/)assets\/replacements\//i.test(candidate) && !/,\s/.test(candidate)) {
                value = candidate;
                return true;
              }
              return false;
            });
          }
          if (value) lock(node, value);
        });
      }
      function schedule() {
        if (scheduled) return;
        scheduled = true;
        requestAnimationFrame(function () { scheduled = false; enforce(); });
      }
      new MutationObserver(schedule).observe(document.documentElement, {
        subtree: true, childList: true, attributes: true,
        attributeFilter: ["src", "poster", "autoplay", "loop", "controls", "data-lazyload", marker].concat(aliases, responsive)
      });
      enforce();
      if (document.readyState === "loading") document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", enforce, { once: true });
      window.addEventListener("load", enforce, { once: true });
      [100, 500, 1500].forEach(function (delay) { setTimeout(enforce, delay); });
    })();
  </script>

<script>
    const slotGames = [
        { name: "Gates of Olympus", provider: "Pragmatic Play", img: "https://0665dd0e20.gphovqlzvc.net/game_pic/square/200/vs20olympx.png", min: 88, max: 98 },
        { name: "Starlight Princess", provider: "Pragmatic Play", img: "https://0665dd0e20.gphovqlzvc.net/game_pic/square/200/vs20starlightx.png", min: 85, max: 97 },
        { name: "Mahjong Ways 2", provider: "PG Soft", img: "https://onelive.dataklmsad902.site/images/game/pg-soft/74.png?s=d03f61d635b298a69b97b68e8949b87b", min: 90, max: 99 },
        { name: "Sweet Bonanza", provider: "Pragmatic Play", img: "https://0665dd0e20.gphovqlzvc.net/game_pic/square/200/vs20fruitswx.png", min: 82, max: 96 },
        { name: "Aztec Gems", provider: "Pragmatic Play", img: "https://0665dd0e20.gphovqlzvc.net/game_pic/square/200/vs5aztecgems.png", min: 80, max: 95 },
        { name: "Great Rhino Megaways", provider: "Pragmatic Play", img: "https://0665dd0e20.gphovqlzvc.net/game_pic/square/200/vswaysrhino.png", min: 86, max: 97 },
        { name: "Mahjong Ways", provider: "PG Soft", img: "https://onelive.dataklmsad902.site/images/game/pg-soft/65.png?s=65b79aceb6571962465d745835dc76ca", min: 85, max: 96 },
        { name: "Lucky Neko", provider: "PG Soft", img: "https://onelive.dataklmsad902.site/images/game/pg-soft/89.png?s=b76a80d04c2714b8116c70160fb64d1b", min: 88, max: 98 },
        { name: "Wild West Gold", provider: "Pragmatic Play", img: "https://0665dd0e20.gphovqlzvc.net/game_pic/square/200/vs40wildwest.png", min: 83, max: 95 },
        { name: "Spaceman", provider: "Pragmatic Play", img: "https://onelive.dataklmsad902.site/images/provider/1/games/bJr6WHB2ULVDoXdV.png?s=045c139f1df95db0e593bf15782e93cc", min: 80, max: 94 },
        { name: "Koi Gate", provider: "Habanero", img: "https://app-a.ply-ldr-rfo6v4aqd6cqw84z.com/img/square/400/SGTheKoiGate.png", min: 89, max: 98 },
        { name: "Gates of Gatot Kaca", provider: "Pragmatic Play", img: "https://onelive.dataklmsad902.site/images/provider/1/games/MCip0vGtWecIdYvu.png?s=eeb9c7a92ef7665c2e0eacb47332ec2a", min: 85, max: 97 }
    ];

    function initRtpSection() {
        const gridContainer = document.getElementById('rtp-grid-container');
        gridContainer.innerHTML = '';

        slotGames.forEach((game, index) => {
            const randomRtp = (Math.random() * (game.max - game.min) + game.min).toFixed(1);
            
            const card = document.createElement('div');
            card.className = 'rtp-card';
            card.innerHTML = `
                <div class="rtp-card-header">
                    <img src="${game.img}" alt="${game.name}">
                    <div class="rtp-game-info">
                        <h3>${game.name}</h3>
                        <span>${game.provider}</span>
                    </div>
                </div>
                <div class="rtp-progress-box">
                    <div class="rtp-percentage-row">
                        <span>RTP LIVE:</span>
                        <span id="rtp-val-${index}">${randomRtp}%</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="rtp-progress-bar-container">
                        <div class="rtp-progress-fill" id="rtp-bar-${index}" style="width: ${randomRtp}%;"></div>
                    </div>
                </div>
                <a href="#" class="rtp-play-btn">> POLA_GACOR_[ON]</a>
            `;
            gridContainer.appendChild(card);
        });
    }

    function updateLiveRtp() {
        slotGames.forEach((game, index) => {
            const valSpan = document.getElementById(`rtp-val-${index}`);
            const barFill = document.getElementById(`rtp-bar-${index}`);
            
            if (valSpan && barFill) {
                let currentVal = parseFloat(valSpan.innerText);
                let fluctuation = (Math.random() * 1.6 - 0.8);
                let newVal = (currentVal + fluctuation);
                
                if (newVal > game.max) newVal = game.max;
                if (newVal < game.min) newVal = game.min;
                
                let finalVal = newVal.toFixed(1) + '%';
                valSpan.innerText = finalVal;
                barFill.style.width = finalVal;
            }
        });
    }

    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
        initRtpSection();
        setInterval(updateLiveRtp, 3500);
    });
</script>

  <style id="transformer-source-first-interface-style" data-transformer-style="source-first-interface">
    [data-transformer-fallback-hidden="true"]{display:none!important}
    [data-transformer-fallback-panel="true"][aria-hidden="false"]{visibility:visible!important;opacity:1!important;height:auto!important;max-height:none!important;overflow:visible!important}
  </style>
  <script id="transformer-source-first-interface-runtime">
    (()=>{
      if(window.__transformerSourceFirstInterface)return;
      window.__transformerSourceFirstInterface=true;
      const before=new WeakMap();
      const beforeQuantity=new WeakMap();
      const panelState=new WeakMap();
      const closest=(target,selector)=>target instanceof Element?target.closest(selector):null;
      const rememberPanel=target=>{
        const remembered=panelState.get(target);
        if(remembered)return remembered;
        const computed=getComputedStyle(target);
        const state={display:computed.display==="none"?"block":computed.display};
        panelState.set(target,state);
        target.setAttribute("data-transformer-fallback-panel","true");
        return state;
      };
      const setPanelState=(target,expanded)=>{
        const state=rememberPanel(target);
        const set=(name,value)=>target.style.setProperty(name,value,"important");
        target.hidden=!expanded;
        target.toggleAttribute("data-transformer-fallback-hidden",!expanded);
        target.setAttribute("aria-hidden",String(!expanded));
        set("display",expanded?state.display:"none");
        set("visibility",expanded?"visible":"hidden");
        set("opacity",expanded?"1":"0");
        set("height",expanded?"auto":"0px");
        set("max-height",expanded?"none":"0px");
        set("overflow",expanded?"visible":"hidden");
      };
      const capture=event=>{
        const control=closest(event.target,"[aria-expanded][aria-controls]");
        if(control)before.set(control,control.getAttribute("aria-expanded"));
        const quantityControl=closest(event.target,"[quantity-control-increase],[data-quantity-increase],[quantity-control-decrease],[data-quantity-decrease]");
        const quantityRoot=quantityControl?.closest("[quantity-control],[data-quantity]");
        const input=quantityRoot?.querySelector('input[name="quantity"],input[type="number"]');
        if(input)beforeQuantity.set(input,Number(input.value)||1);
      };
      document.addEventListener("pointerdown",capture,true);
      document.addEventListener("click",capture,true);
      document.addEventListener("click",event=>{
        const control=closest(event.target,"[aria-expanded][aria-controls]");
        if(control){
          queueMicrotask(()=>{
            const previous=before.get(control);
            const target=document.getElementById(control.getAttribute("aria-controls")||"");
            if(previous===undefined||!target)return;
            const current=control.getAttribute("aria-expanded");
            const expanded=current===previous?previous!=="true":current==="true";
            control.setAttribute("aria-expanded",String(expanded));
            setPanelState(target,expanded);
          });
          return;
        }
        const increase=closest(event.target,"[quantity-control-increase],[data-quantity-increase]");
        const decrease=closest(event.target,"[quantity-control-decrease],[data-quantity-decrease]");
        const quantityRoot=(increase||decrease)?.closest("[quantity-control],[data-quantity]");
        const input=quantityRoot?.querySelector('input[name="quantity"],input[type="number"]');
        if(input){
          const current=beforeQuantity.get(input)??(Number(input.value)||1);
          const next=increase?current+1:Math.max(Number(input.min)||1,current-1);
          queueMicrotask(()=>{
            if(Number(input.value)===current){
              input.value=String(next);
              input.dispatchEvent(new Event("change",{bubbles:true}));
            }
          });
        }
      },false);
    })();
  </script>

  <script data-transformer-shared-asset-loader>
    (()=>{const host=document.getElementById("transformer-shared-assets");if(!host)return;const assets=JSON.parse(host.textContent||"{}");for(const attribute of ["src","data-src","data-lazy","data-lazy-src","data-original","data-original-src","data-image","data-image-src","data-large-image","data-large_image","data-zoom-image","data-o-src","data-o_src","data-o_data-src","data-o_data-large_image","data-flickity-lazyload","data-flickity-lazyload-src","poster","data-lazyload","data-thumb","data-thumb-src","data-hierophant-replacement-src"]){const marker="data-transformer-asset-"+attribute;for(const element of document.querySelectorAll("["+marker+"]")){const key=element.getAttribute(marker);if(key&&assets[key])element.setAttribute(attribute,assets[key]);element.removeAttribute(marker)}}})();

<script id="olx-yesstyle-header-spacing">
(function () {
  function initialize() {
    var header = document.querySelector('header.header-module-scss-module__lXl03a__navWrapper');
    if (!header) return;
    function update() {
      document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--olx-header-height', Math.ceil(header.getBoundingClientRect().height) + 'px');
    }
    update();
    if ('ResizeObserver' in window) new ResizeObserver(update).observe(header);
    else window.addEventListener('resize', update, { passive: true });
  }
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initialize, { once: true });
  else initialize();
}());
</script>
  <script id="olx-yesstyle-review-expansion">
    (function () {
      function initialize() {
        var section = document.querySelector('.customerreviews-module-scss-module__SB6Mqa__customerReview');
        if (!section) return;
        var panel = section.querySelector('.customerreviews-module-scss-module__SB6Mqa__readMoreReview');
        var button = section.querySelector('.customerreviews-module-scss-module__SB6Mqa__loadMore');
        var list = section.querySelector('.customerreviews-module-scss-module__SB6Mqa__customerReviewMasonry');
        if (!panel || !button || !list) return;
        var desktop = window.matchMedia('(min-width: 600px)');
        var items = Array.prototype.filter.call(list.children, function (item) { return item.tagName === 'LI'; });
        var initialHeight = list.style.getPropertyValue('height');
        var initialHeightPriority = list.style.getPropertyPriority('height');
        var frame = 0;
        panel.id = panel.id || 'olx-yesstyle-review-list';
        button.setAttribute('aria-controls', panel.id);
        function syncExpandedState() {
          var expanded = !desktop.matches || section.getAttribute('data-olx-reviews-expanded') === 'true';
          button.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(expanded));
          button.setAttribute('aria-disabled', String(expanded));
        }
        function resizeMasonry() {
          frame = 0;
          if (!desktop.matches) {
            if (initialHeight) list.style.setProperty('height', initialHeight, initialHeightPriority);
            else list.style.removeProperty('height');
            return;
          }
          var layout = window.getComputedStyle(list);
          if (layout.display !== 'flex' || layout.flexDirection !== 'column') return;
          var columns = {};
          var gap = parseFloat(layout.rowGap) || 0;
          items.forEach(function (item) {
            var style = window.getComputedStyle(item);
            if (style.display === 'none') return;
            var order = style.order;
            var height = item.getBoundingClientRect().height + (parseFloat(style.marginTop) || 0) + (parseFloat(style.marginBottom) || 0);
            columns[order] = (columns[order] || 0) + height + (columns[order] ? gap : 0);
          });
          var heights = Object.keys(columns).map(function (key) { return columns[key]; });
          if (!heights.length) return;
          var extra = layout.boxSizing === 'border-box'
            ? (parseFloat(layout.paddingTop) || 0) + (parseFloat(layout.paddingBottom) || 0) + (parseFloat(layout.borderTopWidth) || 0) + (parseFloat(layout.borderBottomWidth) || 0)
            : 0;
          var height = Math.ceil(Math.max.apply(Math, heights) + extra + 1) + 'px';
          if (list.style.getPropertyValue('height') !== height) list.style.setProperty('height', height, 'important');
        }
        function scheduleResize() {
          if (!frame) frame = window.requestAnimationFrame(resizeMasonry);
        }
        button.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
          event.stopPropagation();
          if (!desktop.matches || section.getAttribute('data-olx-reviews-expanded') === 'true') return;
          section.setAttribute('data-olx-reviews-expanded', 'true');
          syncExpandedState();
          scheduleResize();
        });
        desktop.addEventListener('change', function () {
          syncExpandedState();
          scheduleResize();
        });
        window.addEventListener('resize', scheduleResize, { passive: true });
        if ('ResizeObserver' in window) {
          var observer = new ResizeObserver(scheduleResize);
          items.forEach(function (item) { observer.observe(item); });
        }
        list.addEventListener('load', scheduleResize, true);
        syncExpandedState();
        scheduleResize();
      }
      if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initialize, { once: true });
      } else {
        initialize();
      }
    }());
  </script>
